Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the stereochemistry object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 586. The moduli space has dimension at most 315 with 4 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to stereochemistry theory, we decompose the problem into 586 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 315. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
